losing his grip

by okay_so_yeah_713

Summary

Ilya is the youngest-ever captain of the Boston Raiders at 22 years old, and is pretty sure he is straight. Shane was injured before he could go pro, and became a dancer instead. Now, he’s out, and the most in-demand male pole dancer in Montreal.

Then, on a random Friday night, Ilya and the Raiders walk into his club. And now Ilya can’t stop thinking about him.

OR - What if Shane was Ilya’s bi awakening, and it began at a club where Shane was performing? And what happens when this Shane Hollander meets this Ilya Rozanov?

(Don’t worry, Shane still plays hockey for fun in this, because I’ll be damned if Shane isn’t still a bitchy jock who is really good at hockey in my AU.)

Notes

I have been so inspired by reading all the amazing HR fics on this site, that it spurred me to pick up the pen. Clearly, because when you put the bisexual awakening Ilya fries on the tattooed Shane burger… :)

Constructive feedback welcome, but please be kind!

Everything is 100% written by me. I did some internet research to help with locations, injury and hockey stuff, etc., but the writing is AI-free, I promise! I just really love em-dashes.

Also, this takes place towards the start of the 2013-14 season. So, Ilya is in his fourth NHL season and Shane graduated college earlier that Spring.

Inspired by the very talented dancer/choreographer @Kotyyn on Instagram – check them out!

He had to pay double his deposit, but it was worth it to have the pole installed directly into his apartment. Now, he can practice and record videos in his own home, instead of only when the studio is available. 

Sunday and Monday are his two “free” days, and since Sunday is usually busy with beer league and trying to catch up with friends or family, Shane records on Mondays.

Once he’s inside his car, he shoots Jackie a quick text to let her know he’s headed over.

Chapter 3: you make me curious

Chapter Summary

After his interesting evening at the club, Ilya tries to figure out what he’s feeling, and what he’s going to do about it.

Chapter Notes

We are back in Ilya POV! And he is going through it, but it’s all part of the journey. Also, pay attention to months/dates because (in true HR fashion) there are some time skips.

I feel I should mention, I loosely based Ilya’s bisexual self-discovery / some of his inner thoughts on my own. I know that every person has their own experience, and this is just one version. Hopefully some people can relate to it, too!

Please excuse my horrendous photoshop job in this one, but I was trying to avoid using AI to make the image. I think it gets the point across, though.

Ottawa International Airport

He’d been doing a pretty good job of it, too, distracted by getting a team of hungover idiots through the rest of the weekend and winning Monday’s game. A game in which he decidedly did not go easy on the Centaurs, despite being asked to by a very perplexing Metros fan with tattoos and freckles.

It’s not like he usually does much for Thanksgiving, anyways, and they’re in New York. He still doesn’t understand why he responded the way he did to Wolfe’s performance, or perhaps even more strangely, to being so close to him in the alley. And ever since that night, Ilya has been noticing attractive men more than ever before. 

Or rather, he’s noticing the way he notices them. 

Where once he might appraise their looks the way he would a beautiful painting, now as soon as the thought enters his mind, he finds himself wondering… is this even a normal way to look at a hot guy? Are his teammates doing this, too? Do they recognize a man’s beauty the way Ilya does, and does it affect them like it affects him? Do they ever want to reach out and touch?

Fuck it.

Thoughts on Ch7:

Thoughts on Ch8:
- Hilarious that I ever thought I was going to fit this chapter and the next into ONE split POV chapter, just…hilarious.
- While it was tempting to make Shane really upset/mad about Ilya leaving like that, I do think that this more experienced Shane a) could genuinely tell Ilya enjoyed sleeping with him, b) has empathy for his situation as a fellow queer athlete, and c) didn’t expect it to be anything serious to begin with, so… instead he was just like damn, there could be five diff reasons why he left…but it was probs either a sexual identity crisis OR he just doesn’t like me as a person :(
- I simply can’t keep these boys apart lol, they are magnets I fear. I always loved that in the Vegas bathroom scene it was like neither of them wanted to want each other, but they just can’t help it once they’re in the same room. And I had fun inverting the dynamic a little.
- Hope you enjoyed this visit to 2014/15 via songs. Sometimes I feel like a DJ writing this
- Not even kidding, when I started, the dress looked blue/black and by the end it looked white/gold to me – witchcraft!!
- It was kind of (very) sad not writing any smut for this chapter…luckily I get to in the next one!
